# File coverage and reproducible baseline

Enumerated with `rg --files --hidden`; excluded Git internals, dependency/build caches, local secret directory, Python environments, binary GeoIP data, and generated demo reports. Text source, manifests, lockfiles, documentation and tooling were pattern-scanned. A pattern hit is discovery evidence, not manual semantic review. Historical run JSON is not a MySQL benchmark. Source hashes allow later drift detection.
